# H04P evidence / Протокол H04P

Review required; no automatic HW VERIFIED promotion.

## Reviewed disposition

**PASS for the H04-Powered bounded profile scope; verification level: HW OBSERVED.**
The runner recorded all 21 measurement points, all profile guards passed, final operator
OFF confirmation was received, and the raw event log recorded 8,964 events. The only
non-zero `SYST:ERR?` responses were the three intentional direct over-limit probes,
each `120, "Parameter overflowed"`; each probe preserved its channel's 3.30 V setpoint
and was followed by `0, "No error"`. No unexpected instrument errors were recorded.
The panel gate confirmed 4.51 V requests clamped to 4.50 V on all channels. API guards
rejected 4.51 V and 0.501 A before SCPI; power-only setpoint/timer/remote changes were
also rejected before SCPI.

Firmware strings observed in successive `*IDN?` replies were `1.04-1.04` and
`1.04-0.01`; neither is enforced by the library. CH3 `STAT:OPER:INST:ISUM3:COND?`
returned `8` in both CV and CC, while `STAT:QUES:INST:ISUM3:COND?` returned `1` for
CV and `2` for CC, and the independent KEL103 readings agreed with each mode. This is
the recorded CH3 status-bit caveat. The KEL103 is used as an independent comparison,
not as a calibrated reference; unrestricted rated limits were not energized.

Raw command/response and measurement evidence: [H04P event log](../../reports/hardware/2026-10-26T123057.662164Z_H04P/events.jsonl).

### Interrupted attempts and fixes

Three earlier H04P runs stopped safely because runner guard lists had not been updated
when the 0.250 A load changed to CR=11 Ω. The first rejected 11 Ω before sending the
load command; after that guard was fixed, a second guard still refused to enable the
load INPUT until 11 Ω was added to its verified-resistance list. Focused offline tests
now cover configuring 11 Ω and enabling INPUT only after its readback is verified.
These were runner defects, not instrument errors; no unexpected non-zero error queue
response was recorded. See [first stopped attempt](2026-10-26T120214.551777Z_H04P_qualification.md)
and [second stopped attempt](2026-10-26T121716.524429Z_H04P_qualification.md).

A separate restart stopped when a prior panel confirmation token was supplied without
performing the panel attempt in that restarted run. The runner correctly read CH1 still
at 3.30 V and stopped before connecting the load. This was an operator-gate sequence
mistake by the test operator (agent), not an instrument defect; the next run performed
the panel attempt and verified the 4.50 V readback. See [gate-mismatch attempt](2026-10-26T122529.356916Z_H04P_qualification.md).
